CONFIGURATION\n# ================================================================\n\nRESULT_DIR = \"phi_ew_results\"\nos.makedirs(RESULT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n# ------------------------------------------------\n# Physical inputs\n# ------------------------------------------------\n\nALPHA0 = 1.0 / 137.035999084\nGF = 1.1663787e-5             # GeV^-2\nMZ = 91.1876                  # GeV\nMT = 172.76                   # GeV\nMH = 125.25                   # GeV\nALPHAS_MZ = 0.1180\n\n# Experimental MW reference.\n# Keep this value explicit and easy to change.\nMW_EXP = 80.3692              # GeV\n\n# Previously identified crossing scale\nMU_REFERENCE = 60.20          # GeV\n\n# Golden ratio\nPHI = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0\n\n# Algebraic projection\nS2_PHI = 3.0 / (5.0 * PHI**2)\n\n# Numerical domain\nMU_MIN = 1.0\nMU_MAX = 1.0e4\n\n# Number of scan points\nN_SCAN = 600\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 1. BASIC TREE-LEVEL ELECTROWEAK QUANTITIES\n# ================================================================\n\nalpha_em = ALPHA0\ne0 = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * alpha_em)\n\n# On-shell weak angle inferred from MW/MZ\nS2_OS_EXP = 1.0 - MW_EXP**2 / MZ**2\nC2_OS_EXP = 1.0 - S2_OS_EXP\n\n# MW implied directly by the algebraic projection\nMW_PHI_TREE = MZ * math.sqrt(1.0 - S2_PHI)\n\n\ndef delta_r_from_mw(MW, alpha=ALPHA0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Invert Sirlin relation:\n\n        pi alpha /\n        [sqrt(2) GF MW^2 sW^2]\n        = 1 - Delta r\n\n    with\n\n        sW^2 = 1 - MW^2/MZ^2\n    \"\"\"\n\n    s2 = 1.0 - MW**2 / MZ**2\n\n    lhs = math.pi * alpha / (\n        math.sqrt(2.0) * GF * MW**2 * s2\n    )\n\n    return 1.0 - lhs\n\n\n# Delta-r corresponding to experimental MW\nDELTA_R_EXP = delta_r_from_mw(MW_EXP)\n\n# Delta-r required if the algebraic value is interpreted literally\nDELTA_R_PHI_TREE = delta_r_from_mw(MW_PHI_TREE)\n\nDELTA_R_GAP = DELTA_R_PHI_TREE - DELTA_R_EXP\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 2. INITIAL CONDITIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef initial_couplings_at_MZ():\n    \"\"\"\n    Approximate MSbar initial conditions at MZ.\n\n    g1 here is the Standard Model hypercharge coupling g',\n    NOT the GUT-normalized sqrt(5/3) g'.\n\n    This convention is important because\n\n        sW^2 = g'^2/(g^2+g'^2)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    alpha = ALPHA0\n\n    e = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * alpha)\n\n    # Approximate MSbar weak angle near MZ.\n    # This is an INPUT for the RG audit and is deliberately\n    # reported separately from the OS value.\n    s2_msbar_MZ = 0.23122\n\n    c2_msbar_MZ = 1.0 - s2_msbar_MZ\n\n    g2 = e / math.sqrt(s2_msbar_MZ)\n    gp = e / math.sqrt(c2_msbar_MZ)\n\n    g3 = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * ALPHAS_MZ)\n\n    # Approximate running top Yukawa at MZ.\n    # Pole -> running conversion is represented explicitly\n    # by a compact perturbative approximation.\n    yt = math.sqrt(2.0) * MT / 246.21965\n\n    # Higgs quartic tree-level initialization\n    v = 1.0 / math.sqrt(math.sqrt(2.0) * GF)\n    lam = MH**2 / (2.0 * v**2)\n\n    return np.array([\n        gp,\n        g2,\n        g3,\n        yt,\n        lam\n    ], dtype=float)\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 3. ONE-LOOP BETA FUNCTIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_1loop(t, Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    SM one-loop beta functions.\n\n    Y = [g', g2, g3, yt, lambda]\n\n    Convention:\n        dg/dt = beta(g)/(16 pi^2)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    pi16 = 16.0 * math.pi**2\n\n    # Gauge beta coefficients for g', g2, g3\n    b1 = 41.0 / 6.0\n    b2 = -19.0 / 6.0\n    b3 = -7.0\n\n    beta_gp = b1 * gp**3\n    beta_g2 = b2 * g2**3\n    beta_g3 = b3 * g3**3\n\n    # top Yukawa\n    beta_yt = yt * (\n        9.0/2.0 * yt**2\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n        - 8.0 * g3**2\n    )\n\n    # Higgs quartic\n    beta_lam = (\n        24.0 * lam**2\n        - 6.0 * yt**4\n        + 12.0 * lam * yt**2\n        - 9.0 * lam * g2**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * gp**2\n        + 9.0/8.0 * g2**4\n        + 3.0/4.0 * g2**2 * gp**2\n        + 3.0/8.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    return np.array([\n        beta_gp,\n        beta_g2,\n        beta_g3,\n        beta_yt,\n        beta_lam\n    ]) / pi16\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 4. TWO-LOOP GAUGE BETA FUNCTIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_2loop_gauge(Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Two-loop SM gauge contributions.\n\n    The matrix is written for (g', g2, g3).\n\n    This is the non-GUT-normalized hypercharge convention.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    # One-loop\n    b = np.array([\n        41.0/6.0,\n        -19.0/6.0,\n        -7.0\n    ])\n\n    # Two-loop gauge matrix\n    B = np.array([\n        [199.0/18.0, 9.0/2.0, 44.0/3.0],\n        [3.0/2.0, 35.0/6.0, 12.0],\n        [11.0/6.0, 9.0/2.0, -26.0]\n    ])\n\n    g = np.array([gp, g2, g3])\n\n    beta = np.zeros(3)\n\n    for i in range(3):\n        beta[i] += b[i] * g[i]**3\n\n        for j in range(3):\n            beta[i] += (\n                g[i]**3\n                * B[i, j]\n                * g[j]**2\n            )\n\n    # Yukawa contributions to gauge beta functions\n    beta[0] += -(17.0/6.0) * gp**3 * yt**2\n    beta[1] += -(3.0/2.0) * g2**3 * yt**2\n    beta[2] += -2.0 * g3**3 * yt**2\n\n    return beta\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 5. TWO-LOOP YUKAWA + HIGGS APPROXIMATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_2loop_extra(Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compact two-loop approximation for yt and lambda.\n\n    The purpose is stability testing of the crossing, not a\n    replacement for a full precision-electroweak library.\n\n    The gauge sector is retained explicitly at two loops.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    # One-loop pieces\n    beta_yt_1 = yt * (\n        9.0/2.0 * yt**2\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n        - 8.0 * g3**2\n    )\n\n    beta_lam_1 = (\n        24.0 * lam**2\n        - 6.0 * yt**4\n        + 12.0 * lam * yt**2\n        - 9.0 * lam * g2**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * gp**2\n        + 9.0/8.0 * g2**4\n        + 3.0/4.0 * g2**2 * gp**2\n        + 3.0/8.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    # Dominant two-loop terms.\n    # These are deliberately kept explicit so they can be replaced\n    # by a complete Machacek-Vaughn / Buttazzo implementation.\n\n    beta_yt_2 = yt * (\n        -12.0 * yt**4\n        + 6.0 * yt**2 * (\n            17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n            + 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n            + 8.0 * g3**2\n        )\n        - 12.0 * g3**4\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**4\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    beta_lam_2 = (\n        -312.0 * lam**3\n        + 36.0 * lam**2 * yt**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * yt**4\n        + 30.0 * yt**6\n        + 80.0 * g3**2 * yt**4\n        - 8.0 * g3**2 * lam * yt**2\n        - 3.0 * gp**2 * yt**4\n        + 3.0 * gp**2 * lam * yt**2\n        + 3.0 * g2**2 * yt**4\n        - 9.0 * g2**2 * lam * yt**2\n    )\n\n    factor = (16.0 * math.pi**2)**2\n\n    return np.array([\n        beta_yt_2,\n        beta_lam_2\n    ]) / factor\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 6. COMPLETE RG SYSTEM\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_1loop_system(t, Y):\n    return beta_1loop(t, Y)\n\n\ndef beta_2loop_system(t, Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    One-loop + two-loop corrections.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    one = beta_1loop(t, Y)\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    two_gauge = beta_2loop_gauge(Y) / (\n        (16.0 * math.pi**2)**2\n    )\n\n    two_yt_lam = beta_2loop_extra(Y)\n\n    out = one.copy()\n\n    out[:3] += two_gauge\n    out[3:] += two_yt_lam\n\n    return out\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 7. INTEGRATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef integrate_rg(two_loop=True):\n\n    Y0 = initial_couplings_at_MZ()\n\n    beta = beta_2loop_system if two_loop else beta_1loop_system\n\n    # Integrate in t = ln(mu/MZ)\n    t_min = math.log(MU_MIN / MZ)\n    t_max = math.log(MU_MAX / MZ)\n\n    sol = solve_ivp(\n        beta,\n        (t_min, t_max),\n        Y0,\n        dense_output=True,\n        rtol=1e-10,\n        atol=1e-12,\n        max_step=0.03\n    )\n\n    return sol\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 8. WEAK MIXING ANGLE\n# ================================================================\n\ndef s2_running(mu, sol):\n\n    t = math.log(mu / MZ)\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = sol.sol(t)\n\n    return gp**2 / (gp**2 + g2**2)\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 9. FIND CROSSING SCALE\n# ================================================================\n\ndef find_crossing(sol):\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(MU_MIN, MU_MAX, N_SCAN)\n\n    values = np.array([\n        s2_running(mu, sol) - S2_PHI\n        for mu in mus\n    ])\n\n    roots = []\n\n    for i in range(len(mus)-1):\n\n        if values[i] == 0.0:\n            roots.append(mus[i])\n\n        elif values[i] * values[i+1] < 0.0:\n\n            root = brentq(\n                lambda x:\n                    s2_running(x, sol) - S2_PHI,\n                mus[i],\n                mus[i+1]\n            )\n\n            roots.append(root)\n\n    return roots\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 10. THRESHOLD PARAMETERIZATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef finite_threshold_estimate(mu):\n\n    \"\"\"\n    Controlled finite threshold estimate.\n\n    IMPORTANT:\n    This is NOT fitted to MW.\n\n    It is only a diagnostic parametrization showing the size\n    required to shift the running crossing.\n\n    The actual publication calculation should replace this\n    function with the complete two-loop matching coefficients.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Leading logarithmic structure from states above mu.\n    masses = [\n        (\"W\", 80.379),\n        (\"Z\", MZ),\n        (\"H\", MH),\n        (\"t\", MT)\n    ]\n\n    delta = 0.0\n\n    # Small explicit perturbative coefficient.\n    # Kept visible rather than hidden.\n    coeff = 1.0 / (16.0 * math.pi**2)\n\n    for _, mass in masses:\n\n        if mass > mu:\n\n            delta += coeff * math.log(\n                mass**2 / mu**2\n            )\n\n    return delta\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 11. EFFECTIVE MATCHED CROSSING\n# ================================================================\n\ndef find_matched_crossing(sol):\n\n    def f(mu):\n\n        running = s2_running(mu, sol)\n\n        threshold = finite_threshold_estimate(mu)\n\n        return (\n            running\n            - S2_PHI\n            - threshold\n        )\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(MU_MIN, MU_MAX, N_SCAN)\n\n    vals = np.array([f(mu) for mu in mus])\n\n    roots = []\n\n    for i in range(len(mus)-1):\n\n        if vals[i] * vals[i+1] < 0:\n\n            root = brentq(\n                f,\n                mus[i],\n                mus[i+1]\n            )\n\n            roots.append(root)\n\n    return roots\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 12. RUNNING -> MW TEST\n# ================================================================\n\ndef mw_from_s2_tree(s2):\n\n    return MZ * math.sqrt(1.0 - s2)\n\n\ndef mw_from_delta_r(delta_r):\n\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve:\n\n        MW^2 (1 - MW^2/MZ^2)\n        =\n        pi alpha /\n        [sqrt(2) GF (1-Delta_r)]\n    \"\"\"\n\n    target = (\n        math.pi * ALPHA0\n        /\n        (\n            math.sqrt(2.0)\n            * GF\n            * (1.0 - delta_r)\n        )\n    )\n\n    def f(MW):\n\n        return (\n            MW**2\n            * (1.0 - MW**2 / MZ**2)\n            - target\n        )\n\n    return brentq(\n        f,\n        60.0,\n        90.0\n    )\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 13. RUN AUDIT\n# ================================================================\n\ndef run_audit():\n\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n    print(\"PHI ELECTROWEAK TWO-LOOP THRESHOLD AUDIT\")\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n\n    print()\n    print(\"INPUTS\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(f\"phi                 = {PHI:.15f}\")\n    print(f\"s_phi^2             = {S2_PHI:.12f}\")\n    print(f\"G_F                 = {GF:.12e}\")\n    print(f\"M_Z                 = {MZ:.6f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"M_t                 = {MT:.4f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"M_H                 = {MH:.4f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"alpha_s(MZ)         = {ALPHAS_MZ:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"MW experimental ref   = {MW_EXP:.6f} GeV\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\"TREE-LEVEL PHI TEST\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(phi, tree)       = \"\n        f\"{MW_PHI_TREE:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"sW^2(OS, MW_exp)     = \"\n        f\"{S2_OS_EXP:.12f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r(exp MW)      = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_EXP:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r(phi)         = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_PHI_TREE:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r gap          = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_GAP:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 1-loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"1-LOOP RG\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    sol1 = integrate_rg(two_loop=False)\n\n    roots1 = find_crossing(sol1)\n\n    if roots1:\n        mu1 = roots1[0]\n        s1 = s2_running(mu1, sol1)\n\n        print(f\"mu_phi^(1L)         = {mu1:.8f} GeV\")\n        print(f\"sW^2(mu_phi)         = {s1:.12f}\")\n    else:\n        mu1 = np.nan\n        print(\"No 1L crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 2-loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"2-LOOP RG\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    sol2 = integrate_rg(two_loop=True)\n\n    roots2 = find_crossing(sol2)\n\n    if roots2:\n        mu2 = roots2[0]\n        s2 = s2_running(mu2, sol2)\n\n        print(f\"mu_phi^(2L)         = {mu2:.8f} GeV\")\n        print(f\"sW^2(mu_phi)         = {s2:.12f}\")\n    else:\n        mu2 = np.nan\n        print(\"No 2L crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # matched\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"2-LOOP + FINITE THRESHOLD DIAGNOSTIC\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    roots_match = find_matched_crossing(sol2)\n\n    if roots_match:\n        mu_match = roots_match[0]\n\n        s_match = s2_running(\n            mu_match,\n            sol2\n        )\n\n        threshold = finite_threshold_estimate(\n            mu_match\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"mu_phi^(2L+match)   = \"\n            f\"{mu_match:.8f} GeV\"\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"sW^2(running)       = \"\n            f\"{s_match:.12f}\"\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"delta_threshold     = \"\n            f\"{threshold:.12e}\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n        mu_match = np.nan\n        threshold = np.nan\n\n        print(\"No matched crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Stability\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu1) and np.isfinite(mu2):\n\n        delta_mu = mu2 - mu1\n        rel_mu = delta_mu / mu1\n\n    else:\n\n        delta_mu = np.nan\n        rel_mu = np.nan\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu2) and np.isfinite(mu_match):\n\n        delta_mu_match = mu_match - mu2\n        rel_mu_match = delta_mu_match / mu2\n\n    else:\n\n        delta_mu_match = np.nan\n        rel_mu_match = np.nan\n\n    print()\n    print(\"SCALE STABILITY\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(\n        f\"1L -> 2L delta_mu    = \"\n        f\"{delta_mu:.8f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"1L -> 2L relative    = \"\n        f\"{rel_mu:.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"2L -> matching       = \"\n        f\"{delta_mu_match:.8f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"2L -> matching rel.  = \"\n        f\"{rel_mu_match:.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # MW tests\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"M_W TEST\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    MW_1L_TREE = (\n        mw_from_s2_tree(S2_PHI)\n    )\n\n    MW_EXP_DIFF = (\n        MW_1L_TREE - MW_EXP\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(phi tree)         = \"\n        f\"{MW_1L_TREE:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(exp reference)    = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"delta MW             = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP_DIFF:+.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"delta MW / MW        = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP_DIFF / MW_EXP:+.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Classification\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    rg_pass = (\n        np.isfinite(mu2)\n        and abs(mu2 - MU_REFERENCE)\n        / MU_REFERENCE\n        < 0.05\n    )\n\n    threshold_reasonable = (\n        np.isfinite(threshold)\n        and abs(threshold) < 0.01\n    )\n\n    mw_tree_pass = (\n        abs(MW_EXP_DIFF) < 0.01\n    )\n\n    if rg_pass and threshold_reasonable:\n        rg_status = \"PASS\"\n    elif np.isfinite(mu2):\n        rg_status = \"INCONCLUSIVE\"\n    else:\n        rg_status = \"FAIL\"\n\n    mw_status = \"PASS\" if mw_tree_pass else \"FAIL\"\n\n    print()\n    print(\"AUDIT CLASSIFICATION\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(f\"RG crossing           : {rg_status}\")\n    print(f\"MW tree-level         : {mw_status}\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\n        \"IMPORTANT: MW tree-level failure does NOT by itself \"\n        \"falsify the MSbar crossing.\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Summary dataframe\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    summary = pd.DataFrame([{\n\n        \"phi\": PHI,\n        \"s_phi2\": S2_PHI,\n        \"mu_reference_GeV\": MU_REFERENCE,\n        \"mu_phi_1L_GeV\": mu1,\n        \"mu_phi_2L_GeV\": mu2,\n        \"mu_phi_2L_match_GeV\": mu_match,\n        \"delta_mu_1L_to_2L_GeV\": delta_mu,\n        \"relative_shift_1L_to_2L\": rel_mu,\n        \"delta_mu_2L_to_match_GeV\": delta_mu_match,\n        \"relative_shift_2L_to_match\": rel_mu_match,\n        \"delta_threshold\": threshold,\n        \"MW_phi_tree_GeV\": MW_PHI_TREE,\n        \"MW_exp_GeV\": MW_EXP,\n        \"MW_difference_GeV\": MW_EXP_DIFF,\n        \"MW_relative_difference\": MW_EXP_DIFF / MW_EXP,\n        \"s2_OS_exp\": S2_OS_EXP,\n        \"delta_r_exp\": DELTA_R_EXP,\n        \"delta_r_phi\": DELTA_R_PHI_TREE,\n        \"delta_r_gap\": DELTA_R_GAP,\n        \"RG_status\": rg_status,\n        \"MW_tree_status\": mw_status\n    }])\n\n    summary.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_audit_summary.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Running scan\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(\n        MU_MIN,\n        MU_MAX,\n        N_SCAN\n    )\n\n    rows = []\n\n    for mu in mus:\n\n        s1v = s2_running(mu, sol1)\n        s2v = s2_running(mu, sol2)\n        th = finite_threshold_estimate(mu)\n\n        rows.append({\n            \"mu_GeV\": mu,\n            \"s2_1L\": s1v,\n            \"s2_2L\": s2v,\n            \"s_phi2\": S2_PHI,\n            \"difference_1L\": s1v - S2_PHI,\n            \"difference_2L\": s2v - S2_PHI,\n            \"threshold\": th,\n            \"matched_difference\": s2v - S2_PHI - th\n        })\n\n    scan = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\n    scan.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_scan.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Plot\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))\n\n    plt.semilogx(\n        scan[\"mu_GeV\"],\n        scan[\"s2_1L\"],\n        label=\"1-loop RG\"\n    )\n\n    plt.semilogx(\n        scan[\"mu_GeV\"],\n        scan[\"s2_2L\"],\n        label=\"2-loop RG\"\n    )\n\n    plt.axhline(\n        S2_PHI,\n        linestyle=\"--\",\n        label=r\"$3/(5\\phi^2)$\"\n    )\n\n    plt.axvline(\n        MU_REFERENCE,\n        linestyle=\":\",\n        label=\"60.20 GeV reference\"\n    )\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu2):\n        plt.axvline(\n            mu2,\n            linestyle=\"--\",\n            label=\"2-loop crossing\"\n        )\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu_match):\n        plt.axvline(\n            mu_match,\n            linestyle=\"-.\",\n            label=\"2L + threshold\"\n        )\n\n    plt.xlabel(r\"$\\mu$ [GeV]\")\n    plt.ylabel(r\"$s_W^2(\\mu)$\")\n\n    plt.title(\n        \"Electroweak Running and Golden-Ratio Projection\"\n    )\n\n    plt.legend()\n    plt.grid(True, alpha=0.3)\n    plt.tight_layout()\n\n    plt.savefig(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_running.png\"\n        ),\n        dpi=180\n    )\n\n    plt.close()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Sensitivity analysis\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    global MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ\n\n    sensitivity_rows = []\n\n    variations = {\n        \"central\": (MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mt_minus\": (MT - 1.0, MH, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mt_plus\": (MT + 1.0, MH, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mh_minus\": (MT, MH - 1.0, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mh_plus\": (MT, MH + 1.0, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"alpha_s_minus\": (MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ - 0.001),\n        \"alpha_s_plus\": (MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ + 0.001)\n    }\n\n    original_MT = MT\n    original_MH = MH\n    original_AS = ALPHAS_MZ\n\n    for label, values in variations.items():\n\n        MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ = values\n\n        try:\n            sol = integrate_rg(two_loop=True)\n            roots = find_crossing(sol)\n            mu_root = roots[0] if roots else np.nan\n        except Exception:\n            mu_root = np.nan\n\n        sensitivity_rows.append({\n            \"variation\": label,\n            \"Mt_GeV\": MT,\n            \"MH_GeV\": MH,\n            \"alpha_s\": ALPHAS_MZ,\n            \"mu_phi_2L_GeV\": mu_root\n        })\n\n    # Restore\n    MT = original_MT\n    MH = original_MH\n    ALPHAS_MZ = original_AS\n\n    sensitivity = pd.DataFrame(sensitivity_rows)\n\n    sensitivity.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_sensitivity.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # JSON\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    result_json = {\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"phi\": PHI,\n            \"s_phi_squared\": S2_PHI,\n            \"GF\": GF,\n            \"MZ_GeV\": MZ,\n            \"Mt_GeV\": MT,\n            \"MH_GeV\": MH,\n            \"alpha_s_MZ\": ALPHAS_MZ,\n            \"MW_exp_GeV\": MW_EXP\n        },\n        \"tree_level\": {\n            \"MW_phi_GeV\": MW_PHI_TREE,\n            \"s2_OS_exp\": S2_OS_EXP,\n            \"delta_r_exp\": DELTA_R_EXP,\n            \"delta_r_phi\": DELTA_R_PHI_TREE,\n            \"delta_r_gap\": DELTA_R_GAP\n        },\n        \"RG\": {\n            \"mu_phi_1L_GeV\": None if not np.isfinite(mu1) else float(mu1),\n            \"mu_phi_2L_GeV\": None if not np.isfinite(mu2) else float(mu2),\n            \"mu_phi_2L_match_GeV\": None if not np.isfinite(mu_match) else float(mu_match),\n            \"relative_shift_1L_to_2L\": None if not np.isfinite(rel_mu) else float(rel_mu),\n            \"relative_shift_2L_to_matching\": None if not np.isfinite(rel_mu_match) else float(rel_mu_match)\n        },\n        \"classification\": {\n            \"RG\": rg_status,\n            \"MW_tree\": mw_status\n        }\n    }\n\n    with open(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_audit.json\"\n        ),\n        \"w\"\n    ) as f:\n        json.dump(result_json, f, indent=2)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Report\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    report = f\"\"\"\n======================================================================\nPHI ELECTROWEAK TWO-LOOP THRESHOLD AUDIT\n======================================================================\n\nGOLDEN-RATIO PROJECTION\n-----------------------\nphi                     = {PHI:.15f}\ns_phi^2                 = {S2_PHI:.12f}\n\nREFERENCE SCALE\n---------------\nmu_phi reference        = {MU_REFERENCE:.6f} GeV\n\nTREE-LEVEL TEST\n---------------\nMW(phi)                 = {MW_PHI_TREE:.8f} GeV\nMW(exp reference)       = {MW_EXP:.8f} GeV\ndelta MW                = {MW_EXP_DIFF:+.8f} GeV\n\nON-SHELL TEST\n-------------\nsW^2(OS, MWexp)         = {S2_OS_EXP:.12f}\n\nDelta r(exp)            = {DELTA_R_EXP:.10f}\nDelta r(phi)            = {DELTA_R_PHI_TREE:.10f}\nDelta r gap             = {DELTA_R_GAP:.10f}\n\nRG CROSSING\n-----------\nmu_phi 1-loop           = {mu1}\nmu_phi 2-loop           = {mu2}\nmu_phi 2L + threshold   = {mu_match}\n\nSCALE SHIFTS\n------------\n1L -> 2L                = {delta_mu} GeV\nrelative                = {rel_mu}\n\n2L -> matching          = {delta_mu_match} GeV\nrelative                = {rel_mu_match}\n\nTHRESHOLD\n---------\ndelta_threshold         = {threshold}\n\nCLASSIFICATION\n--------------\nRG                      = {rg_status}\nMW tree                 = {mw_status}\n\nINTERPRETATION\n--------------\nThe algebraic projection s_phi^2 is NOT treated as an on-shell\nmeasurement of sin^2(theta_W).\n\nThe physically meaningful test is whether the MSbar crossing near\n60.20 GeV survives:\n\n  1. two-loop RG evolution,\n  2. finite threshold matching,\n  3. MSbar -> on-shell conversion,\n  4. prediction of MW.\n\nThe finite threshold term in this script is a diagnostic\nparametrization and must be replaced by complete two-loop\nelectroweak matching coefficients for a publication-grade result.\n\n======================================================================\n\"\"\"\n\n    with open(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_report.txt\"\n        ),\n        \"w\"\n    ) as f:\n        f.write(report)\n\n    print()\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n    print(\"FILES GENERATED\")\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n\n    for filename in os.listdir(RESULT_DIR):\n        print(\n            os.path.join(\n                RESULT_DIR,\n                filename\n            )\n        )\n\n    print()\n    print(report)\n\n    return {\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"scan\": scan,\n        \"sensitivity\": sensitivity,\n        \"report\": report\n    }\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 14. EXECUTE\n# ================================================================\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    results = run_audit()","metadata":{"trusted":true,"execution":{"iopub.status.busy":"2026-08-25T12:50:17.771652Z","iopub.execute_input":"2026-08-25T12:50:17.772234Z","iopub.status.idle":"2026-08-25T12:50:17.826398Z","shell.execute_reply.started":"2026-08-25T12:50:17.772207Z","shell.execute_reply":"2026-08-25T12:50:17.825432Z"}},"outputs":[{"traceback":["\u001b[0;36m  File \u001b[0;32m\"/tmp/ipykernel_58/2820400416.py\"\u001b[0;36m, line \u001b[0;32m997\u001b[0m\n\u001b[0;31m    global MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ\u001b[0m\n\u001b[0m    ^\u001b[0m\n\u001b[0;31mSyntaxError\u001b[0m\u001b[0;31m:\u001b[0m name 'MT' is used prior to global declaration\n"],"ename":"SyntaxError","evalue":"name 'MT' is used prior to global declaration (2820400416.py, line 997)","output_type":"error"}],"execution_count":15},{"cell_type":"code","source":"# ================================================================\n# ASTRA-Q / PHI ELECTROWEAK AUDIT\n# TWO-LOOP RG + THRESHOLD + ON-SHELL MW TEST\n#\n# Purpose:\n#    Test whether\n#\n#        s_phi^2 = 3/(5 phi^2)\n#\n#    corresponds to a physically meaningful electroweak scale\n#    near the previously identified\n#\n#        mu_phi ~ 60.20 GeV\n#\n# IMPORTANT:\n#    This script is an explicit perturbative audit.\n#    It does NOT fit delta_threshold to force agreement.\n#\n# Outputs:\n#    results/\n#        phi_ew_audit_summary.csv\n#        phi_ew_scan.csv\n#        phi_ew_sensitivity.csv\n#        phi_ew_audit.json\n#        phi_ew_running.png\n#        phi_ew_report.txt\n# ================================================================\n\nimport os\nimport json\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\nfrom scipy.integrate import solve_ivp\nfrom scipy.optimize import brentq\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 0. CONFIGURATION\n# ================================================================\n\nRESULT_DIR = \"phi_ew_results\"\nos.makedirs(RESULT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n# ------------------------------------------------\n# Physical inputs\n# ------------------------------------------------\n\nALPHA0 = 1.0 / 137.035999084\nGF = 1.1663787e-5             # GeV^-2\nMZ = 91.1876                  # GeV\nMT = 172.76                   # GeV\nMH = 125.25                   # GeV\nALPHAS_MZ = 0.1180\n\n# Experimental MW reference.\n# Keep this value explicit and easy to change.\nMW_EXP = 80.3692              # GeV\n\n# Previously identified crossing scale\nMU_REFERENCE = 60.20          # GeV\n\n# Golden ratio\nPHI = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0\n\n# Algebraic projection\nS2_PHI = 3.0 / (5.0 * PHI**2)\n\n# Numerical domain\nMU_MIN = 1.0\nMU_MAX = 1.0e4\n\n# Number of scan points\nN_SCAN = 600\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 1. BASIC TREE-LEVEL ELECTROWEAK QUANTITIES\n# ================================================================\n\nalpha_em = ALPHA0\ne0 = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * alpha_em)\n\n# On-shell weak angle inferred from MW/MZ\nS2_OS_EXP = 1.0 - MW_EXP**2 / MZ**2\nC2_OS_EXP = 1.0 - S2_OS_EXP\n\n# MW implied directly by the algebraic projection\nMW_PHI_TREE = MZ * math.sqrt(1.0 - S2_PHI)\n\n\ndef delta_r_from_mw(MW, alpha=ALPHA0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Invert Sirlin relation:\n\n        pi alpha /\n        [sqrt(2) GF MW^2 sW^2]\n        = 1 - Delta r\n\n    with\n\n        sW^2 = 1 - MW^2/MZ^2\n    \"\"\"\n\n    s2 = 1.0 - MW**2 / MZ**2\n\n    lhs = math.pi * alpha / (\n        math.sqrt(2.0) * GF * MW**2 * s2\n    )\n\n    return 1.0 - lhs\n\n\n# Delta-r corresponding to experimental MW\nDELTA_R_EXP = delta_r_from_mw(MW_EXP)\n\n# Delta-r required if the algebraic value is interpreted literally\nDELTA_R_PHI_TREE = delta_r_from_mw(MW_PHI_TREE)\n\nDELTA_R_GAP = DELTA_R_PHI_TREE - DELTA_R_EXP\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 2. INITIAL CONDITIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef initial_couplings_at_MZ():\n    \"\"\"\n    Approximate MSbar initial conditions at MZ.\n\n    g1 here is the Standard Model hypercharge coupling g',\n    NOT the GUT-normalized sqrt(5/3) g'.\n\n    This convention is important because\n\n        sW^2 = g'^2/(g^2+g'^2)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    alpha = ALPHA0\n\n    e = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * alpha)\n\n    # Approximate MSbar weak angle near MZ.\n    # This is an INPUT for the RG audit and is deliberately\n    # reported separately from the OS value.\n    s2_msbar_MZ = 0.23122\n\n    c2_msbar_MZ = 1.0 - s2_msbar_MZ\n\n    g2 = e / math.sqrt(s2_msbar_MZ)\n    gp = e / math.sqrt(c2_msbar_MZ)\n\n    g3 = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * ALPHAS_MZ)\n\n    # Approximate running top Yukawa at MZ.\n    # Pole -> running conversion is represented explicitly\n    # by a compact perturbative approximation.\n    yt = math.sqrt(2.0) * MT / 246.21965\n\n    # Higgs quartic tree-level initialization\n    v = 1.0 / math.sqrt(math.sqrt(2.0) * GF)\n    lam = MH**2 / (2.0 * v**2)\n\n    return np.array([\n        gp,\n        g2,\n        g3,\n        yt,\n        lam\n    ], dtype=float)\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 3. ONE-LOOP BETA FUNCTIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_1loop(t, Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    SM one-loop beta functions.\n\n    Y = [g', g2, g3, yt, lambda]\n\n    Convention:\n        dg/dt = beta(g)/(16 pi^2)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    pi16 = 16.0 * math.pi**2\n\n    # Gauge beta coefficients for g', g2, g3\n    b1 = 41.0 / 6.0\n    b2 = -19.0 / 6.0\n    b3 = -7.0\n\n    beta_gp = b1 * gp**3\n    beta_g2 = b2 * g2**3\n    beta_g3 = b3 * g3**3\n\n    # top Yukawa\n    beta_yt = yt * (\n        9.0/2.0 * yt**2\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n        - 8.0 * g3**2\n    )\n\n    # Higgs quartic\n    beta_lam = (\n        24.0 * lam**2\n        - 6.0 * yt**4\n        + 12.0 * lam * yt**2\n        - 9.0 * lam * g2**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * gp**2\n        + 9.0/8.0 * g2**4\n        + 3.0/4.0 * g2**2 * gp**2\n        + 3.0/8.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    return np.array([\n        beta_gp,\n        beta_g2,\n        beta_g3,\n        beta_yt,\n        beta_lam\n    ]) / pi16\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 4. TWO-LOOP GAUGE BETA FUNCTIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_2loop_gauge(Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Two-loop SM gauge contributions.\n\n    The matrix is written for (g', g2, g3).\n\n    This is the non-GUT-normalized hypercharge convention.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    # One-loop\n    b = np.array([\n        41.0/6.0,\n        -19.0/6.0,\n        -7.0\n    ])\n\n    # Two-loop gauge matrix\n    B = np.array([\n        [199.0/18.0, 9.0/2.0, 44.0/3.0],\n        [3.0/2.0, 35.0/6.0, 12.0],\n        [11.0/6.0, 9.0/2.0, -26.0]\n    ])\n\n    g = np.array([gp, g2, g3])\n\n    beta = np.zeros(3)\n\n    for i in range(3):\n        beta[i] += b[i] * g[i]**3\n\n        for j in range(3):\n            beta[i] += (\n                g[i]**3\n                * B[i, j]\n                * g[j]**2\n            )\n\n    # Yukawa contributions to gauge beta functions\n    beta[0] += -(17.0/6.0) * gp**3 * yt**2\n    beta[1] += -(3.0/2.0) * g2**3 * yt**2\n    beta[2] += -2.0 * g3**3 * yt**2\n\n    return beta\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 5. TWO-LOOP YUKAWA + HIGGS APPROXIMATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_2loop_extra(Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compact two-loop approximation for yt and lambda.\n\n    The purpose is stability testing of the crossing, not a\n    replacement for a full precision-electroweak library.\n\n    The gauge sector is retained explicitly at two loops.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    # One-loop pieces\n    beta_yt_1 = yt * (\n        9.0/2.0 * yt**2\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n        - 8.0 * g3**2\n    )\n\n    beta_lam_1 = (\n        24.0 * lam**2\n        - 6.0 * yt**4\n        + 12.0 * lam * yt**2\n        - 9.0 * lam * g2**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * gp**2\n        + 9.0/8.0 * g2**4\n        + 3.0/4.0 * g2**2 * gp**2\n        + 3.0/8.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    # Dominant two-loop terms.\n    # These are deliberately kept explicit so they can be replaced\n    # by a complete Machacek-Vaughn / Buttazzo implementation.\n\n    beta_yt_2 = yt * (\n        -12.0 * yt**4\n        + 6.0 * yt**2 * (\n            17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n            + 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n            + 8.0 * g3**2\n        )\n        - 12.0 * g3**4\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**4\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    beta_lam_2 = (\n        -312.0 * lam**3\n        + 36.0 * lam**2 * yt**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * yt**4\n        + 30.0 * yt**6\n        + 80.0 * g3**2 * yt**4\n        - 8.0 * g3**2 * lam * yt**2\n        - 3.0 * gp**2 * yt**4\n        + 3.0 * gp**2 * lam * yt**2\n        + 3.0 * g2**2 * yt**4\n        - 9.0 * g2**2 * lam * yt**2\n    )\n\n    factor = (16.0 * math.pi**2)**2\n\n    return np.array([\n        beta_yt_2,\n        beta_lam_2\n    ]) / factor\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 6. COMPLETE RG SYSTEM\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_1loop_system(t, Y):\n    return beta_1loop(t, Y)\n\n\ndef beta_2loop_system(t, Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    One-loop + two-loop corrections.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    one = beta_1loop(t, Y)\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    two_gauge = beta_2loop_gauge(Y) / (\n        (16.0 * math.pi**2)**2\n    )\n\n    two_yt_lam = beta_2loop_extra(Y)\n\n    out = one.copy()\n\n    out[:3] += two_gauge\n    out[3:] += two_yt_lam\n\n    return out\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 7. INTEGRATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef integrate_rg(two_loop=True):\n\n    Y0 = initial_couplings_at_MZ()\n\n    beta = beta_2loop_system if two_loop else beta_1loop_system\n\n    # Integrate in t = ln(mu/MZ)\n    t_min = math.log(MU_MIN / MZ)\n    t_max = math.log(MU_MAX / MZ)\n\n    sol = solve_ivp(\n        beta,\n        (t_min, t_max),\n        Y0,\n        dense_output=True,\n        rtol=1e-10,\n        atol=1e-12,\n        max_step=0.03\n    )\n\n    return sol\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 8. WEAK MIXING ANGLE\n# ================================================================\n\ndef s2_running(mu, sol):\n\n    t = math.log(mu / MZ)\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = sol.sol(t)\n\n    return gp**2 / (gp**2 + g2**2)\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 9. FIND CROSSING SCALE\n# ================================================================\n\ndef find_crossing(sol):\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(MU_MIN, MU_MAX, N_SCAN)\n\n    values = np.array([\n        s2_running(mu, sol) - S2_PHI\n        for mu in mus\n    ])\n\n    roots = []\n\n    for i in range(len(mus)-1):\n\n        if values[i] == 0.0:\n            roots.append(mus[i])\n\n        elif values[i] * values[i+1] < 0.0:\n\n            root = brentq(\n                lambda x:\n                    s2_running(x, sol) - S2_PHI,\n                mus[i],\n                mus[i+1]\n            )\n\n            roots.append(root)\n\n    return roots\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 10. THRESHOLD PARAMETERIZATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef finite_threshold_estimate(mu):\n\n    \"\"\"\n    Controlled finite threshold estimate.\n\n    IMPORTANT:\n    This is NOT fitted to MW.\n\n    It is only a diagnostic parametrization showing the size\n    required to shift the running crossing.\n\n    The actual publication calculation should replace this\n    function with the complete two-loop matching coefficients.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Leading logarithmic structure from states above mu.\n    masses = [\n        (\"W\", 80.379),\n        (\"Z\", MZ),\n        (\"H\", MH),\n        (\"t\", MT)\n    ]\n\n    delta = 0.0\n\n    # Small explicit perturbative coefficient.\n    # Kept visible rather than hidden.\n    coeff = 1.0 / (16.0 * math.pi**2)\n\n    for _, mass in masses:\n\n        if mass > mu:\n\n            delta += coeff * math.log(\n                mass**2 / mu**2\n            )\n\n    return delta\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 11. EFFECTIVE MATCHED CROSSING\n# ================================================================\n\ndef find_matched_crossing(sol):\n\n    def f(mu):\n\n        running = s2_running(mu, sol)\n\n        threshold = finite_threshold_estimate(mu)\n\n        return (\n            running\n            - S2_PHI\n            - threshold\n        )\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(MU_MIN, MU_MAX, N_SCAN)\n\n    vals = np.array([f(mu) for mu in mus])\n\n    roots = []\n\n    for i in range(len(mus)-1):\n\n        if vals[i] * vals[i+1] < 0:\n\n            root = brentq(\n                f,\n                mus[i],\n                mus[i+1]\n            )\n\n            roots.append(root)\n\n    return roots\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 12. RUNNING -> MW TEST\n# ================================================================\n\ndef mw_from_s2_tree(s2):\n\n    return MZ * math.sqrt(1.0 - s2)\n\n\ndef mw_from_delta_r(delta_r):\n\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve:\n\n        MW^2 (1 - MW^2/MZ^2)\n        =\n        pi alpha /\n        [sqrt(2) GF (1-Delta_r)]\n    \"\"\"\n\n    target = (\n        math.pi * ALPHA0\n        /\n        (\n            math.sqrt(2.0)\n            * GF\n            * (1.0 - delta_r)\n        )\n    )\n\n    def f(MW):\n\n        return (\n            MW**2\n            * (1.0 - MW**2 / MZ**2)\n            - target\n        )\n\n    return brentq(\n        f,\n        60.0,\n        90.0\n    )\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 13. RUN AUDIT\n# ================================================================\n\ndef run_audit():\n\n    global MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ\n\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n    print(\"PHI ELECTROWEAK TWO-LOOP THRESHOLD AUDIT\")\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n\n    print()\n    print(\"INPUTS\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(f\"phi                 = {PHI:.15f}\")\n    print(f\"s_phi^2             = {S2_PHI:.12f}\")\n    print(f\"G_F                 = {GF:.12e}\")\n    print(f\"M_Z                 = {MZ:.6f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"M_t                 = {MT:.4f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"M_H                 = {MH:.4f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"alpha_s(MZ)         = {ALPHAS_MZ:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"MW experimental ref   = {MW_EXP:.6f} GeV\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\"TREE-LEVEL PHI TEST\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(phi, tree)       = \"\n        f\"{MW_PHI_TREE:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"sW^2(OS, MW_exp)     = \"\n        f\"{S2_OS_EXP:.12f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r(exp MW)      = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_EXP:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r(phi)         = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_PHI_TREE:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r gap          = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_GAP:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 1-loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"1-LOOP RG\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    sol1 = integrate_rg(two_loop=False)\n\n    roots1 = find_crossing(sol1)\n\n    if roots1:\n        mu1 = roots1[0]\n        s1 = s2_running(mu1, sol1)\n\n        print(f\"mu_phi^(1L)         = {mu1:.8f} GeV\")\n        print(f\"sW^2(mu_phi)         = {s1:.12f}\")\n    else:\n        mu1 = np.nan\n        print(\"No 1L crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 2-loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"2-LOOP RG\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    sol2 = integrate_rg(two_loop=True)\n\n    roots2 = find_crossing(sol2)\n\n    if roots2:\n        mu2 = roots2[0]\n        s2 = s2_running(mu2, sol2)\n\n        print(f\"mu_phi^(2L)         = {mu2:.8f} GeV\")\n        print(f\"sW^2(mu_phi)         = {s2:.12f}\")\n    else:\n        mu2 = np.nan\n        print(\"No 2L crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # matched\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"2-LOOP + FINITE THRESHOLD DIAGNOSTIC\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    roots_match = find_matched_crossing(sol2)\n\n    if roots_match:\n        mu_match = roots_match[0]\n\n        s_match = s2_running(\n            mu_match,\n            sol2\n        )\n\n        threshold = finite_threshold_estimate(\n            mu_match\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"mu_phi^(2L+match)   = \"\n            f\"{mu_match:.8f} GeV\"\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"sW^2(running)       = \"\n            f\"{s_match:.12f}\"\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"delta_threshold     = \"\n            f\"{threshold:.12e}\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n        mu_match = np.nan\n        threshold = np.nan\n\n        print(\"No matched crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Stability\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu1) and np.isfinite(mu2):\n\n        delta_mu = mu2 - mu1\n        rel_mu = delta_mu / mu1\n\n    else:\n\n        delta_mu = np.nan\n        rel_mu = np.nan\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu2) and np.isfinite(mu_match):\n\n        delta_mu_match = mu_match - mu2\n        rel_mu_match = delta_mu_match / mu2\n\n    else:\n\n        delta_mu_match = np.nan\n        rel_mu_match = np.nan\n\n    print()\n    print(\"SCALE STABILITY\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(\n        f\"1L -> 2L delta_mu    = \"\n        f\"{delta_mu:.8f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"1L -> 2L relative    = \"\n        f\"{rel_mu:.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"2L -> matching       = \"\n        f\"{delta_mu_match:.8f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"2L -> matching rel.  = \"\n        f\"{rel_mu_match:.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # MW tests\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"M_W TEST\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    MW_1L_TREE = (\n        mw_from_s2_tree(S2_PHI)\n    )\n\n    MW_EXP_DIFF = (\n        MW_1L_TREE - MW_EXP\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(phi tree)         = \"\n        f\"{MW_1L_TREE:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(exp reference)    = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"delta MW             = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP_DIFF:+.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"delta MW / MW        = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP_DIFF / MW_EXP:+.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Classification\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    rg_pass = (\n        np.isfinite(mu2)\n        and abs(mu2 - MU_REFERENCE)\n        / MU_REFERENCE\n        < 0.05\n    )\n\n    threshold_reasonable = (\n        np.isfinite(threshold)\n        and abs(threshold) < 0.01\n    )\n\n    mw_tree_pass = (\n        abs(MW_EXP_DIFF) < 0.01\n    )\n\n    if rg_pass and threshold_reasonable:\n        rg_status = \"PASS\"\n    elif np.isfinite(mu2):\n        rg_status = \"INCONCLUSIVE\"\n    else:\n        rg_status = \"FAIL\"\n\n    mw_status = \"PASS\" if mw_tree_pass else \"FAIL\"\n\n    print()\n    print(\"AUDIT CLASSIFICATION\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(f\"RG crossing           : {rg_status}\")\n    print(f\"MW tree-level         : {mw_status}\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\n        \"IMPORTANT: MW tree-level failure does NOT by itself \"\n        \"falsify the MSbar crossing.\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Summary dataframe\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    summary = pd.DataFrame([{\n\n        \"phi\": PHI,\n        \"s_phi2\": S2_PHI,\n        \"mu_reference_GeV\": MU_REFERENCE,\n        \"mu_phi_1L_GeV\": mu1,\n        \"mu_phi_2L_GeV\": mu2,\n        \"mu_phi_2L_match_GeV\": mu_match,\n        \"delta_mu_1L_to_2L_GeV\": delta_mu,\n        \"relative_shift_1L_to_2L\": rel_mu,\n        \"delta_mu_2L_to_match_GeV\": delta_mu_match,\n        \"relative_shift_2L_to_match\": rel_mu_match,\n        \"delta_threshold\": threshold,\n        \"MW_phi_tree_GeV\": MW_PHI_TREE,\n        \"MW_exp_GeV\": MW_EXP,\n        \"MW_difference_GeV\": MW_EXP_DIFF,\n        \"MW_relative_difference\": MW_EXP_DIFF / MW_EXP,\n        \"s2_OS_exp\": S2_OS_EXP,\n        \"delta_r_exp\": DELTA_R_EXP,\n        \"delta_r_phi\": DELTA_R_PHI_TREE,\n        \"delta_r_gap\": DELTA_R_GAP,\n        \"RG_status\": rg_status,\n        \"MW_tree_status\": mw_status\n    }])\n\n    summary.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_audit_summary.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Running scan\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(\n        MU_MIN,\n        MU_MAX,\n        N_SCAN\n    )\n\n    rows = []\n\n    for mu in mus:\n\n        s1v = s2_running(mu, sol1)\n        s2v = s2_running(mu, sol2)\n        th = finite_threshold_estimate(mu)\n\n        rows.append({\n            \"mu_GeV\": mu,\n            \"s2_1L\": s1v,\n            \"s2_2L\": s2v,\n            \"s_phi2\": S2_PHI,\n            \"difference_1L\": s1v - S2_PHI,\n            \"difference_2L\": s2v - S2_PHI,\n            \"threshold\": th,\n            \"matched_difference\": s2v - S2_PHI - th\n        })\n\n    scan = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\n    scan.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_scan.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Plot\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))\n\n    plt.semilogx(\n        scan[\"mu_GeV\"],\n        scan[\"s2_1L\"],\n        label=\"1-loop RG\"\n    )\n\n    plt.semilogx(\n        scan[\"mu_GeV\"],\n        scan[\"s2_2L\"],\n        label=\"2-loop RG\"\n    )\n\n    plt.axhline(\n        S2_PHI,\n        linestyle=\"--\",\n        label=r\"$3/(5\\phi^2)$\"\n    )\n\n    plt.axvline(\n        MU_REFERENCE,\n        linestyle=\":\",\n        label=\"60.20 GeV reference\"\n    )\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu2):\n        plt.axvline(\n            mu2,\n            linestyle=\"--\",\n            label=\"2-loop crossing\"\n        )\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu_match):\n        plt.axvline(\n            mu_match,\n            linestyle=\"-.\",\n            label=\"2L + threshold\"\n        )\n\n    plt.xlabel(r\"$\\mu$ [GeV]\")\n    plt.ylabel(r\"$s_W^2(\\mu)$\")\n\n    plt.title(\n        \"Electroweak Running and Golden-Ratio Projection\"\n    )\n\n    plt.legend()\n    plt.grid(True, alpha=0.3)\n    plt.tight_layout()\n\n    plt.savefig(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_running.png\"\n        ),\n        dpi=180\n    )\n\n    plt.close()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Sensitivity analysis\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    sensitivity_rows = []\n\n    variations = {\n        \"central\": (MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mt_minus\": (MT - 1.0, MH, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mt_plus\": (MT + 1.0, MH, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mh_minus\": (MT, MH - 1.0, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"mh_plus\": (MT, MH + 1.0, ALPHAS_MZ),\n        \"alpha_s_minus\": (MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ - 0.001),\n        \"alpha_s_plus\": (MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ + 0.001)\n    }\n\n    original_MT = MT\n    original_MH = MH\n    original_AS = ALPHAS_MZ\n\n    for label, values in variations.items():\n\n        MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ = values\n\n        try:\n            sol = integrate_rg(two_loop=True)\n            roots = find_crossing(sol)\n            mu_root = roots[0] if roots else np.nan\n        except Exception:\n            mu_root = np.nan\n\n        sensitivity_rows.append({\n            \"variation\": label,\n            \"Mt_GeV\": MT,\n            \"MH_GeV\": MH,\n            \"alpha_s\": ALPHAS_MZ,\n            \"mu_phi_2L_GeV\": mu_root\n        })\n\n    # Restore\n    MT = original_MT\n    MH = original_MH\n    ALPHAS_MZ = original_AS\n\n    sensitivity = pd.DataFrame(sensitivity_rows)\n\n    sensitivity.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_sensitivity.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # JSON\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    result_json = {\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"phi\": PHI,\n            \"s_phi_squared\": S2_PHI,\n            \"GF\": GF,\n            \"MZ_GeV\": MZ,\n            \"Mt_GeV\": MT,\n            \"MH_GeV\": MH,\n            \"alpha_s_MZ\": ALPHAS_MZ,\n            \"MW_exp_GeV\": MW_EXP\n        },\n        \"tree_level\": {\n            \"MW_phi_GeV\": MW_PHI_TREE,\n            \"s2_OS_exp\": S2_OS_EXP,\n            \"delta_r_exp\": DELTA_R_EXP,\n            \"delta_r_phi\": DELTA_R_PHI_TREE,\n            \"delta_r_gap\": DELTA_R_GAP\n        },\n        \"RG\": {\n            \"mu_phi_1L_GeV\": None if not np.isfinite(mu1) else float(mu1),\n            \"mu_phi_2L_GeV\": None if not np.isfinite(mu2) else float(mu2),\n            \"mu_phi_2L_match_GeV\": None if not np.isfinite(mu_match) else float(mu_match),\n            \"relative_shift_1L_to_2L\": None if not np.isfinite(rel_mu) else float(rel_mu),\n            \"relative_shift_2L_to_matching\": None if not np.isfinite(rel_mu_match) else float(rel_mu_match)\n        },\n        \"classification\": {\n            \"RG\": rg_status,\n            \"MW_tree\": mw_status\n        }\n    }\n\n    with open(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_audit.json\"\n        ),\n        \"w\"\n    ) as f:\n        json.dump(result_json, f, indent=2)\n\n    # MSbar -> on-shell conversion,\n  4. prediction of MW.\n\nThe finite threshold term in this script is a diagnostic\nparametrization and must be replaced by complete two-loop\nelectroweak matching coefficients for a publication-grade result.\n\n======================================================================\n\"\"\"\n\n    with open(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_report.txt\"\n        ),\n        \"w\"\n    ) as f:\n        f.write(report)\n\n    print()\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n    print(\"FILES GENERATED\")\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n\n    for filename in os.listdir(RESULT_DIR):\n        print(\n            os.path.join(\n                RESULT_DIR,\n                filename\n            )\n        )\n\n    print()\n    print(report)\n\n    return {\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"scan\": scan,\n        \"sensitivity\": sensitivity,\n        \"report\": report\n    }\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 14. CONFIGURATION\n# ================================================================\n\nRESULT_DIR = \"phi_ew_results\"\nos.makedirs(RESULT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n# ------------------------------------------------\n# Physical inputs\n# ------------------------------------------------\n\nALPHA0 = 1.0 / 137.035999084\nGF = 1.1663787e-5             # GeV^-2\nMZ = 91.1876                  # GeV\nMT = 172.76                   # GeV\nMH = 125.25                   # GeV\nALPHAS_MZ = 0.1180\n\n# Experimental MW reference.\n# Keep this value explicit and easy to change.\nMW_EXP = 80.3692              # GeV\n\n# Previously identified crossing scale\nMU_REFERENCE = 60.20          # GeV\n\n# Golden ratio\nPHI = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0\n\n# Algebraic projection\nS2_PHI = 3.0 / (5.0 * PHI**2)\n\n# Numerical domain (amplified to capture clean 1L/2L crossing)\nMU_MIN = 10.0\nMU_MAX = 1.0e5\n\n# Number of scan points\nN_SCAN = 1000\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 1. BASIC TREE-LEVEL ELECTROWEAK QUANTITIES\n# ================================================================\n\nalpha_em = ALPHA0\ne0 = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * alpha_em)\n\n# On-shell weak angle inferred from MW/MZ\nS2_OS_EXP = 1.0 - MW_EXP**2 / MZ**2\nC2_OS_EXP = 1.0 - S2_OS_EXP\n\n# MW implied directly by the algebraic projection\nMW_PHI_TREE = MZ * math.sqrt(1.0 - S2_PHI)\n\n\ndef delta_r_from_mw(MW, alpha=ALPHA0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Invert Sirlin relation:\n\n        pi alpha /\n        [sqrt(2) GF MW^2 sW^2]\n        = 1 - Delta r\n\n    with\n\n        sW^2 = 1 - MW^2/MZ^2\n    \"\"\"\n\n    s2 = 1.0 - MW**2 / MZ**2\n\n    lhs = math.pi * alpha / (\n        math.sqrt(2.0) * GF * MW**2 * s2\n    )\n\n    return 1.0 - lhs\n\n\n# Delta-r corresponding to experimental MW\nDELTA_R_EXP = delta_r_from_mw(MW_EXP)\n\n# Delta-r required if the algebraic value is interpreted literally\nDELTA_R_PHI_TREE = delta_r_from_mw(MW_PHI_TREE)\n\nDELTA_R_GAP = DELTA_R_PHI_TREE - DELTA_R_EXP\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 2. INITIAL CONDITIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef initial_couplings_at_MZ():\n    \"\"\"\n    Approximate MSbar initial conditions at MZ.\n\n    g1 here is the Standard Model hypercharge coupling g',\n    NOT the GUT-normalized sqrt(5/3) g'.\n\n    This convention is important because\n\n        sW^2 = g'^2/(g^2+g'^2)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    alpha = ALPHA0\n\n    e = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * alpha)\n\n    # Approximate MSbar weak angle near MZ.\n    # This is an INPUT for the RG audit and is deliberately\n    # reported separately from the OS value.\n    s2_msbar_MZ = 0.23122\n\n    c2_msbar_MZ = 1.0 - s2_msbar_MZ\n\n    g2 = e / math.sqrt(s2_msbar_MZ)\n    gp = e / math.sqrt(c2_msbar_MZ)\n\n    g3 = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * ALPHAS_MZ)\n\n    # Approximate running top Yukawa at MZ.\n    # Pole -> running conversion is represented explicitly\n    # by a compact perturbative approximation.\n    yt = math.sqrt(2.0) * MT / 246.21965\n\n    # Higgs quartic tree-level initialization\n    v = 1.0 / math.sqrt(math.sqrt(2.0) * GF)\n    lam = MH**2 / (2.0 * v**2)\n\n    return np.array([\n        gp,\n        g2,\n        g3,\n        yt,\n        lam\n    ], dtype=float)\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 3. ONE-LOOP BETA FUNCTIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_1loop(t, Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    SM one-loop beta functions.\n\n    Y = [g', g2, g3, yt, lambda]\n\n    Convention:\n        dg/dt = beta(g)/(16 pi^2)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    pi16 = 16.0 * math.pi**2\n\n    # Gauge beta coefficients for g', g2, g3\n    b1 = 41.0 / 6.0\n    b2 = -19.0 / 6.0\n    b3 = -7.0\n\n    beta_gp = b1 * gp**3\n    beta_g2 = b2 * g2**3\n    beta_g3 = b3 * g3**3\n\n    # top Yukawa\n    beta_yt = yt * (\n        9.0/2.0 * yt**2\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n        - 8.0 * g3**2\n    )\n\n    # Higgs quartic\n    beta_lam = (\n        24.0 * lam**2\n        - 6.0 * yt**4\n        + 12.0 * lam * yt**2\n        - 9.0 * lam * g2**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * gp**2\n        + 9.0/8.0 * g2**4\n        + 3.0/4.0 * g2**2 * gp**2\n        + 3.0/8.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    return np.array([\n        beta_gp,\n        beta_g2,\n        beta_g3,\n        beta_yt,\n        beta_lam\n    ]) / pi16\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 4. TWO-LOOP GAUGE BETA FUNCTIONS\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_2loop_gauge(Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Two-loop SM gauge contributions.\n\n    The matrix is written for (g', g2, g3).\n\n    This is the non-GUT-normalized hypercharge convention.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    # One-loop\n    b = np.array([\n        41.0/6.0,\n        -19.0/6.0,\n        -7.0\n    ])\n\n    # Two-loop gauge matrix\n    B = np.array([\n        [199.0/18.0, 9.0/2.0, 44.0/3.0],\n        [3.0/2.0, 35.0/6.0, 12.0],\n        [11.0/6.0, 9.0/2.0, -26.0]\n    ])\n\n    g = np.array([gp, g2, g3])\n\n    beta = np.zeros(3)\n\n    for i in range(3):\n        beta[i] += b[i] * g[i]**3\n\n        for j in range(3):\n            beta[i] += (\n                g[i]**3\n                * B[i, j]\n                * g[j]**2\n            )\n\n    # Yukawa contributions to gauge beta functions\n    beta[0] += -(17.0/6.0) * gp**3 * yt**2\n    beta[1] += -(3.0/2.0) * g2**3 * yt**2\n    beta[2] += -2.0 * g3**3 * yt**2\n\n    return beta\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 5. TWO-LOOP YUKAWA + HIGGS APPROXIMATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_2loop_extra(Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compact two-loop approximation for yt and lambda.\n\n    The purpose is stability testing of the crossing, not a\n    replacement for a full precision-electroweak library.\n\n    The gauge sector is retained explicitly at two loops.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    # One-loop pieces\n    beta_yt_1 = yt * (\n        9.0/2.0 * yt**2\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n        - 8.0 * g3**2\n    )\n\n    beta_lam_1 = (\n        24.0 * lam**2\n        - 6.0 * yt**4\n        + 12.0 * lam * yt**2\n        - 9.0 * lam * g2**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * gp**2\n        + 9.0/8.0 * g2**4\n        + 3.0/4.0 * g2**2 * gp**2\n        + 3.0/8.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    # Dominant two-loop terms.\n    # These are deliberately kept explicit so they can be replaced\n    # by a complete Machacek-Vaughn / Buttazzo implementation.\n\n    beta_yt_2 = yt * (\n        -12.0 * yt**4\n        + 6.0 * yt**2 * (\n            17.0/12.0 * gp**2\n            + 9.0/4.0 * g2**2\n            + 8.0 * g3**2\n        )\n        - 12.0 * g3**4\n        - 9.0/4.0 * g2**4\n        - 17.0/12.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    beta_lam_2 = (\n        -312.0 * lam**3\n        + 36.0 * lam**2 * yt**2\n        - 3.0 * lam * yt**4\n        + 30.0 * yt**6\n        + 80.0 * g3**2 * yt**4\n        - 8.0 * g3**2 * lam * yt**2\n        - 3.0 * gp**2 * yt**4\n        + 3.0 * gp**2 * lam * yt**2\n        + 3.0 * g2**2 * yt**4\n        - 9.0 * g2**2 * lam * yt**2\n    )\n\n    factor = (16.0 * math.pi**2)**2\n\n    return np.array([\n        beta_yt_2,\n        beta_lam_2\n    ]) / factor\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 6. COMPLETE RG SYSTEM\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_1loop_system(t, Y):\n    return beta_1loop(t, Y)\n\n\ndef beta_2loop_system(t, Y):\n    \"\"\"\n    One-loop + two-loop corrections.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    one = beta_1loop(t, Y)\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n\n    two_gauge = beta_2loop_gauge(Y) / (\n        (16.0 * math.pi**2)**2\n    )\n\n    two_yt_lam = beta_2loop_extra(Y)\n\n    out = one.copy()\n\n    out[:3] += two_gauge\n    out[3:] += two_yt_lam\n\n    return out\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 7. INTEGRATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef integrate_rg(two_loop=True):\n\n    Y0 = initial_couplings_at_MZ()\n\n    beta = beta_2loop_system if two_loop else beta_1loop_system\n\n    # Integrate in t = ln(mu/MZ)\n    t_min = math.log(MU_MIN / MZ)\n    t_max = math.log(MU_MAX / MZ)\n\n    sol = solve_ivp(\n        beta,\n        (t_min, t_max),\n        Y0,\n        dense_output=True,\n        rtol=1e-10,\n        atol=1e-12,\n        max_step=0.03\n    )\n\n    return sol\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 8. WEAK MIXING ANGLE\n# ================================================================\n\ndef s2_running(mu, sol):\n\n    t = math.log(mu / MZ)\n\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = sol.sol(t)\n\n    return gp**2 / (gp**2 + g2**2)\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 9. FIND CROSSING SCALE\n# ================================================================\n\ndef find_crossing(sol):\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(MU_MIN, MU_MAX, N_SCAN)\n\n    values = np.array([\n        s2_running(mu, sol) - S2_PHI\n        for mu in mus\n    ])\n\n    roots = []\n\n    for i in range(len(mus)-1):\n\n        if values[i] == 0.0:\n            roots.append(mus[i])\n\n        elif values[i] * values[i+1] < 0.0:\n\n            root = brentq(\n                lambda x:\n                    s2_running(x, sol) - S2_PHI,\n                mus[i],\n                mus[i+1]\n            )\n\n            roots.append(root)\n\n    return roots\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 10. THRESHOLD PARAMETERIZATION\n# ================================================================\n\ndef finite_threshold_estimate(mu):\n\n    \"\"\"\n    Controlled finite threshold estimate.\n\n    IMPORTANT:\n    This is NOT fitted to MW.\n\n    It is only a diagnostic parametrization showing the size\n    required to shift the running crossing.\n\n    The actual publication calculation should replace this\n    function with the complete two-loop matching coefficients.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Leading logarithmic structure from states above mu.\n    masses = [\n        (\"W\", 80.379),\n        (\"Z\", MZ),\n        (\"H\", MH),\n        (\"t\", MT)\n    ]\n\n    delta = 0.0\n\n    # Small explicit perturbative coefficient.\n    # Kept visible rather than hidden.\n    coeff = 1.0 / (16.0 * math.pi**2)\n\n    for _, mass in masses:\n\n        if mass > mu:\n\n            delta += coeff * math.log(\n                mass**2 / mu**2\n            )\n\n    return delta\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 11. EFFECTIVE MATCHED CROSSING\n# ================================================================\n\ndef find_matched_crossing(sol):\n\n    def f(mu):\n\n        running = s2_running(mu, sol)\n\n        threshold = finite_threshold_estimate(mu)\n\n        return (\n            running\n            - S2_PHI\n            - threshold\n        )\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(MU_MIN, MU_MAX, N_SCAN)\n\n    vals = np.array([f(mu) for mu in mus])\n\n    roots = []\n\n    for i in range(len(mus)-1):\n\n        if vals[i] * vals[i+1] < 0:\n\n            root = brentq(\n                f,\n                mus[i],\n                mus[i+1]\n            )\n\n            roots.append(root)\n\n    return roots\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 12. RUNNING -> MW TEST\n# ================================================================\n\ndef mw_from_s2_tree(s2):\n\n    return MZ * math.sqrt(1.0 - s2)\n\n\ndef mw_from_delta_r(delta_r):\n\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve:\n\n        MW^2 (1 - MW^2/MZ^2)\n        =\n        pi alpha /\n        [sqrt(2) GF (1-Delta_r)]\n    \"\"\"\n\n    target = (\n        math.pi * ALPHA0\n        /\n        (\n            math.sqrt(2.0)\n            * GF\n            * (1.0 - delta_r)\n        )\n    )\n\n    def f(MW):\n\n        return (\n            MW**2\n            * (1.0 - MW**2 / MZ**2)\n            - target\n        )\n\n    return brentq(\n        f,\n        60.0,\n        90.0\n    )\n\n\n# ================================================================\n# 13. RUN AUDIT\n# ================================================================\n\ndef run_audit():\n\n    global MT, MH, ALPHAS_MZ\n\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n    print(\"PHI ELECTROWEAK TWO-LOOP THRESHOLD AUDIT\")\n    print(\"=\" * 72)\n\n    print()\n    print(\"INPUTS\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(f\"phi                 = {PHI:.15f}\")\n    print(f\"s_phi^2             = {S2_PHI:.12f}\")\n    print(f\"G_F                 = {GF:.12e}\")\n    print(f\"M_Z                 = {MZ:.6f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"M_t                 = {MT:.4f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"M_H                 = {MH:.4f} GeV\")\n    print(f\"alpha_s(MZ)         = {ALPHAS_MZ:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"MW experimental ref   = {MW_EXP:.6f} GeV\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\"TREE-LEVEL PHI TEST\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(phi, tree)       = \"\n        f\"{MW_PHI_TREE:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"sW^2(OS, MW_exp)     = \"\n        f\"{S2_OS_EXP:.12f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r(exp MW)      = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_EXP:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r(phi)         = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_PHI_TREE:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"Delta r gap          = \"\n        f\"{DELTA_R_GAP:.8f}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 1-loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"1-LOOP RG\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    sol1 = integrate_rg(two_loop=False)\n\n    roots1 = find_crossing(sol1)\n\n    if roots1:\n        mu1 = roots1[0]\n        s1 = s2_running(mu1, sol1)\n\n        print(f\"mu_phi^(1L)         = {mu1:.8f} GeV\")\n        print(f\"sW^2(mu_phi)         = {s1:.12f}\")\n    else:\n        mu1 = np.nan\n        print(\"No 1L crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 2-loop\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"2-LOOP RG\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    sol2 = integrate_rg(two_loop=True)\n\n    roots2 = find_crossing(sol2)\n\n    if roots2:\n        mu2 = roots2[0]\n        s2 = s2_running(mu2, sol2)\n\n        print(f\"mu_phi^(2L)         = {mu2:.8f} GeV\")\n        print(f\"sW^2(mu_phi)         = {s2:.12f}\")\n    else:\n        mu2 = np.nan\n        print(\"No 2L crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # matched\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"2-LOOP + FINITE THRESHOLD DIAGNOSTIC\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    roots_match = find_matched_crossing(sol2)\n\n    if roots_match:\n        mu_match = roots_match[0]\n\n        s_match = s2_running(\n            mu_match,\n            sol2\n        )\n\n        threshold = finite_threshold_estimate(\n            mu_match\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"mu_phi^(2L+match)   = \"\n            f\"{mu_match:.8f} GeV\"\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"sW^2(running)       = \"\n            f\"{s_match:.12f}\"\n        )\n\n        print(\n            f\"delta_threshold     = \"\n            f\"{threshold:.12e}\"\n        )\n\n    else:\n        mu_match = np.nan\n        threshold = np.nan\n\n        print(\"No matched crossing found.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Stability\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu1) and np.isfinite(mu2):\n\n        delta_mu = mu2 - mu1\n        rel_mu = delta_mu / mu1\n\n    else:\n\n        delta_mu = np.nan\n        rel_mu = np.nan\n\n    if np.isfinite(mu2) and np.isfinite(mu_match):\n\n        delta_mu_match = mu_match - mu2\n        rel_mu_match = delta_mu_match / mu2\n\n    else:\n\n        delta_mu_match = np.nan\n        rel_mu_match = np.nan\n\n    print()\n    print(\"SCALE STABILITY\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(\n        f\"1L -> 2L delta_mu    = \"\n        f\"{delta_mu:.8f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"1L -> 2L relative    = \"\n        f\"{rel_mu:.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"2L -> matching       = \"\n        f\"{delta_mu_match:.8f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"2L -> matching rel.  = \"\n        f\"{rel_mu_match:.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # MW tests\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    print()\n    print(\"M_W TEST\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    MW_1L_TREE = (\n        mw_from_s2_tree(S2_PHI)\n    )\n\n    MW_EXP_DIFF = (\n        MW_1L_TREE - MW_EXP\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(phi tree)         = \"\n        f\"{MW_1L_TREE:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"MW(exp reference)    = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP:.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"delta MW             = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP_DIFF:+.6f} GeV\"\n    )\n\n    print(\n        f\"delta MW / MW        = \"\n        f\"{MW_EXP_DIFF / MW_EXP:+.6e}\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Classification\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    rg_pass = (\n        np.isfinite(mu2)\n        and abs(mu2 - MU_REFERENCE)\n        / MU_REFERENCE\n        < 0.05\n    )\n\n    threshold_reasonable = (\n        np.isfinite(threshold)\n        and abs(threshold) < 0.01\n    )\n\n    mw_tree_pass = (\n        abs(MW_EXP_DIFF) < 0.01\n    )\n\n    if rg_pass and threshold_reasonable:\n        rg_status = \"PASS\"\n    elif np.isfinite(mu2):\n        rg_status = \"INCONCLUSIVE\"\n    else:\n        rg_status = \"FAIL\"\n\n    mw_status = \"PASS\" if mw_tree_pass else \"FAIL\"\n\n    print()\n    print(\"AUDIT CLASSIFICATION\")\n    print(\"-\" * 72)\n\n    print(f\"RG crossing           : {rg_status}\")\n    print(f\"MW tree-level         : {mw_status}\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\n        \"IMPORTANT: MW tree-level failure does NOT by itself \"\n        \"falsify the MSbar crossing.\"\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Summary dataframe\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    summary = pd.DataFrame([{\n\n        \"phi\": PHI,\n        \"s_phi2\": S2_PHI,\n        \"mu_reference_GeV\": MU_REFERENCE,\n        \"mu_phi_1L_GeV\": mu1,\n        \"mu_phi_2L_GeV\": mu2,\n        \"mu_phi_2L_match_GeV\": mu_match,\n        \"delta_mu_1L_to_2L_GeV\": delta_mu,\n        \"relative_shift_1L_to_2L\": rel_mu,\n        \"delta_mu_2L_to_match_GeV\": delta_mu_match,\n        \"relative_shift_2L_to_match\": rel_mu_match,\n        \"delta_threshold\": threshold,\n        \"MW_phi_tree_GeV\": MW_PHI_TREE,\n        \"MW_exp_GeV\": MW_EXP,\n        \"MW_difference_GeV\": MW_EXP_DIFF,\n        \"MW_relative_difference\": MW_EXP_DIFF / MW_EXP,\n        \"s2_OS_exp\": S2_OS_EXP,\n        \"delta_r_exp\": DELTA_R_EXP,\n        \"delta_r_phi\": DELTA_R_PHI_TREE,\n        \"delta_r_gap\": DELTA_R_GAP,\n        \"RG_status\": rg_status,\n        \"MW_tree_status\": mw_status\n    }])\n\n    summary.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_audit_summary.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Running scan\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    mus = np.geomspace(\n        MU_MIN,\n        MU_MAX,\n        N_SCAN\n    )\n\n    rows = []\n\n    for mu in mus:\n\n        s1v = s2_running(mu, sol1)\n        s2v = s2_running(mu, sol2)\n        th = finite_threshold_estimate(mu)\n\n        rows.append({\n            \"mu_GeV\": mu,\n            \"s2_1L\": s1v,\n            \"s2_2L\": s2v,\n            \"s_phi2\": S2_PHI,\n            \"difference_1L\": s1v - S2_PHI,\n            \"difference_2L\": s2v - S2_PHI,\n            \"threshold\": th,\n            \"matched_difference\": s2v - S2_PHI - th\n        })\n\n    scan = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n\n    scan.to_csv(\n        os.path.join(\n            RESULT_DIR,\n            \"phi_ew_scan.csv\"\n        ),\n        index=False\n    )\n\n    # CONFIGURATION\n# ================================================================\n\nRESULT_DIR = \"phi_ew_results\"\nos.makedirs(RESULT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\nALPHA0 = 1.0 / 137.035999084\nGF = 1.1663787e-5             # GeV^-2\nMZ = 91.1876                  # GeV\nMT = 172.76                   # GeV\nMH = 125.25                   # GeV\nALPHAS_MZ = 0.1180\n\nMW_EXP = 80.3692              # GeV\nMU_REFERENCE = 60.20          # GeV\n\nPHI = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0\nS2_PHI = 3.0 / (5.0 * PHI**2)\n\nMU_MIN = 10.0\nMU_MAX = 1.0e5\nN_SCAN = 1000\n\n# ================================================================\n# 1. INITIAL CONDITIONS (MSbar at MZ)\n# ================================================================\n\ndef initial_couplings_at_MZ():\n    alpha = ALPHA0\n    e = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * alpha)\n    \n    s2_msbar_MZ = 0.23122\n    c2_msbar_MZ = 1.0 - s2_msbar_MZ\n\n    g2 = e / math.sqrt(s2_msbar_MZ)\n    gp = e / math.sqrt(c2_msbar_MZ)\n    g3 = math.sqrt(4.0 * math.pi * ALPHAS_MZ)\n\n    yt = math.sqrt(2.0) * MT / 246.21965\n    v = 1.0 / math.sqrt(math.sqrt(2.0) * GF)\n    lam = MH**2 / (2.0 * v**2)\n\n    return np.array([gp, g2, g3, yt, lam], dtype=float)\n\n# ================================================================\n# 2. BETA FUNCTIONS (1-LOOP & 2-LOOP)\n# ================================================================\n\ndef beta_1loop(t, Y):\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n    pi16 = 16.0 * math.pi**2\n\n    b1, b2, b3 = 41.0 / 6.0, -19.0 / 6.0, -7.0\n\n    beta_gp = b1 * gp**3\n    beta_g2 = b2 * g2**3\n    beta_g3 = b3 * g3**3\n\n    beta_yt = yt * (\n        9.0/2.0 * yt**2 - 17.0/12.0 * gp**2 - 9.0/4.0 * g2**2 - 8.0 * g3**2\n    )\n\n    beta_lam = (\n        24.0 * lam**2 - 6.0 * yt**4 + 12.0 * lam * yt**2\n        - 9.0 * lam * g2**2 - 3.0 * lam * gp**2\n        + 9.0/8.0 * g2**4 + 3.0/4.0 * g2**2 * gp**2 + 3.0/8.0 * gp**4\n    )\n\n    return np.array([beta_gp, beta_g2, beta_g3, beta_yt, beta_lam]) / pi16\n\ndef beta_2loop_gauge(Y):\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n    b = np.array([41.0/6.0, -19.0/6.0, -7.0])\n    B = np.array([\n        [199.0/18.0, 9.0/2.0, 44.0/3.0],\n        [3.0/2.0, 35.0/6.0, 12.0],\n        [11.0/6.0, 9.0/2.0, -26.0]\n    ])\n    g = np.array([gp, g2, g3])\n    beta = np.zeros(3)\n    for i in range(3):\n        beta[i] += b[i] * g[i]**3\n        for j in range(3):\n            beta[i] += g[i]**3 * B[i, j] * g[j]**2\n\n    beta[0] += -(17.0/6.0) * gp**3 * yt**2\n    beta[1] += -(3.0/2.0) * g2**3 * yt**2\n    beta[2] += -2.0 * g3**3 * yt**2\n    return beta\n\ndef beta_2loop_extra(Y):\n    gp, g2, g3, yt, lam = Y\n    beta_yt_2 = yt * (\n        -12.0 * yt**4 + 6.0 * yt**2 * (17.0/12.0 * gp**2 + 9.0/4.0 * g2**2 + 8.0 * g3**2)\n        -
